{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"aims","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"to take aim","type":"example"},{"text":"Take time with the aim of your gun.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The pointing of a weapon, as a gun, a dart, or an arrow, or object, in the line of direction with the object intended to be struck; the line of fire; the direction of anything, such as a spear, a blow, a discourse, a remark, towards a particular point or object, with a view to strike or affect it."]},{"examples":[{"text":"A rifle has more precise aim than a shotgun.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The level of accuracy of a weapon."]},{"glosses":["The point intended to be hit, or object intended to be attained or affected."]},{"examples":[{"text":"My number one aim in life is to make money to make my parents, siblings, and kids happy.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Intention or goal."],"synonyms":[{"word":"purpose"},{"word":"design"},{"word":"scheme"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The police officer has excellent aim, always hitting the bullseye in shooting practice.","type":"example"},{"text":"He has a bad aim, so he decided to join archery classes.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The ability of someone to aim straight; one’s faculty for being able to hit a physical target."]},{"glosses":["Conjecture; guess."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/eɪm/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"intention","word":"aspiration"},{"word":"design"},{"word":"end"},{"word":"ettle"},{"word":"intention"},{"word":"mint"},{"word":"object"},{"word":"purpose"},{"word":"scheme"},{"word":"scope"},{"word":"tendency"}],"upstream_line":174151,"upstream_offset":821191017,"upstream_record_sha256":"65539e0ac753cfb60bc1a9c888756d9f0c552d604a2b38a05f7767b9b160d9fb","word":"aim"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"aims","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"aiming","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"aimed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"aimed","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He aimed at the target, but the arrow flew straight over it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To point or direct a missile, or a weapon which propels as missile, towards an object or spot with the intent of hitting it"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to aim at a pass","type":"example"},{"text":"to aim to do well in life","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To direct the intention or purpose; to attempt the accomplishment of a purpose; to try to gain; to endeavor;—followed by at, or by an infinitive"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to aim an arrow at the deer","type":"example"},{"text":"She aimed a punch at her ex-boyfriend.","type":"example"},{"text":"He aimed the gun at him and he held his hands up in surrender.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To direct or point (e.g. a weapon), at a particular object; to direct, as a missile, an act, or a proceeding, at, to, or against an object"],"synonyms":[{"word":"point"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to aim a satirical comment at Communists in general","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To direct (something verbal) towards a certain person, thing, or group"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To guess or conjecture."],"tags":["intransitive","obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/eɪm/"}],"upstream_line":174152,"upstream_offset":821222039,"upstream_record_sha256":"644558d92838430316e2c0e124b104e6e85df9fb1ebc8cf3bd4098819aaa25bc","word":"aim"}
